  <ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2026-01-01_2026-03-31" name="us-gaap:OrganizationConsolidationAndPresentationOfFinancialStatementsDisclosureTextBlock" id="c3192791" escape="true">
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"><b><a id="notes" href="#"></a>Note <em style="font: inherit;">1.</em> Description of Business</b></p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"><b><i>Organization and Description of Business</i></b></p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;text-indent:25pt;">SeaStar Medical Holding Corporation, a Delaware corporation (&#8220;SeaStar&#8221;) and its wholly owned subsidiary, SeaStar Medical, Inc., are collectively referred to as the &#8220;Company&#8221;. SeaStar Medical, Inc. was incorporated as a Delaware corporation in <em style="font: inherit;"> June 2007, </em>and it is headquartered in Denver, Colorado.&#160;The Company is principally engaged in the research, development, and commercialization of a platform medical device technology designed to modulate inflammation in various patient populations.&#160;The Company received FDA approval for QUELIMMUNE, its commercial pediatric Selective Cytopheretic Device ("SCD") therapy on <em style="font: inherit;"> February 21, 2024, </em>under a Humanitarian Device Exemption (&#8220;HDE&#8221;).&#160;It is the only FDA approved product for use in pediatric patients with acute kidney injury (&#8220;AKI&#8221;) due to sepsis or a septic condition requiring kidney replacement therapy.&#160;In addition, the Company is currently conducting a pivotal clinical trial to assess the safety and efficacy of the SCD therapy in critically ill adult patients with AKI requiring continuous renal replacement therapy (&#8220;CRRT&#8221;).</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;text-indent:25pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;text-indent:25pt;">The initial target indications for&#160;this technology have been&#160;for the treatment of patients with&#160;AKI, but through additional Breakthrough Device Designation (&#8220;BDD&#8221;) from the Food and Drug Administration (&#8220;FDA&#8221;), the Company has expanded development of the SCD therapy into treatments for&#160;patients with cardiorenal syndrome awaiting left ventricular assist device implantation, patients with hepatorenal syndrome, patients with end stage renal disease and adult and pediatric patients undergoing cardiac surgery. The BDD enables the potential for a speedier pathway to approval and the ability to have more frequent and flexible meetings with the FDA.</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b><i>Basis of Presentation and Consolidation</i></b></p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;text-indent:25pt;">The accompanying unaudited condensed consolidated financial statements have been prepared in conformity with accounting principles generally accepted in the United States of America (&#8220;U.S. GAAP&#8221;) and the rules and regulations of the Securities and Exchange Commission (&#8220;SEC&#8221;) for interim reporting. As permitted under those rules and regulations, certain notes or other financial information normally included in financial statements prepared in accordance with U.S. GAAP have been condensed or omitted. The interim unaudited condensed consolidated financial statements have been prepared on the same basis as the annual consolidated financial statements and, in the opinion of management, reflect all adjustments, which include only normal, recurring adjustments that are necessary to present fairly the Company&#8217;s results for the interim periods presented. The results from operations for the <em style="font: inherit;">three</em> months ended <em style="font: inherit;"> March 31, 2026</em>, are <em style="font: inherit;">not</em> necessarily indicative of the results to be expected for the year ending <em style="font: inherit;"> December 31, 2026</em>, or for any future annual or interim period.</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;text-indent:25pt;">The accompanying interim unaudited condensed consolidated financial statements should be read in conjunction with the annual consolidated financial statements and the related notes for the year ended <em style="font: inherit;"> December 31, 2025</em>. There have been <em style="font: inherit;">no</em> material changes in our significant accounting policies as described in our Annual Report on Form <em style="font: inherit;">10</em>-K for the year ended <em style="font: inherit;"> December 31, 2025</em>.</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;text-indent:25pt;">The interim unaudited condensed consolidated financial statements include the consolidated accounts of the Company&#8217;s wholly owned subsidiary, SeaStar Medical, Inc. All significant intercompany transactions have been eliminated in consolidation.&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family: Times New Roman; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; text-align: justify; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt -1pt; text-indent: 26pt;">On <em style="font: inherit;"> June 7, 2024, </em>the Company effected a <em style="font: inherit;">1</em>-for-<ix:nonFraction id="c145591112" contextRef="d_2024-06-07_2024-06-07_NonmonetaryTransactionTypeAxis-ReverseStockSplitMember" unitRef="Pure" name="us-gaap:StockholdersEquityNoteStockSplitConversionRatio1" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">25</ix:nonFraction> reverse-stock split (the <em style="font: inherit;">&#8220;2024</em> Reverse Stock Split&#8221;) of its issued and outstanding shares of common stock, par value $<ix:nonFraction id="c145591114" contextRef="i_2024-06-07" unitRef="USDPerShare" name="us-gaap:CommonStockParOrStatedValuePerShare" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">0.0001</ix:nonFraction> (the &#8220;common stock&#8221;). Following the effect of the <em style="font: inherit;">2024</em> Reverse Stock Split, each <ix:nonFraction id="c145591116" contextRef="d_2024-06-07_2024-06-07_NonmonetaryTransactionTypeAxis-ReverseStockSplitMember" unitRef="Pure" name="us-gaap:StockholdersEquityNoteStockSplitConversionRatio1" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">25</ix:nonFraction> shares of the Company&#8217;s common stock that were issued and outstanding automatically converted into <em style="font: inherit;">one</em> outstanding share of common stock.</p>
   <p style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt -9pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family: Times New Roman; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; text-align: justify; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt -1pt; text-indent: 26pt;">On <em style="font: inherit;"> January 5, 2026, </em>the Company elected a <em style="font: inherit;">1</em>-for-<ix:nonFraction id="c145591119" contextRef="d_2026-01-05_2026-01-05_NonmonetaryTransactionTypeAxis-ReverseStockSplitMember" unitRef="Pure" name="us-gaap:StockholdersEquityNoteStockSplitConversionRatio1" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">10</ix:nonFraction> reverse-stock split (the <em style="font: inherit;">&#8220;2026</em> Reverse Stock Split,&#8221; together with the <em style="font: inherit;">2024</em> Reverse Stock Split, the &#8220;Reverse Stock Splits&#8221;) of its issued and outstanding shares of common stock, par value $<ix:nonFraction id="c145591122" contextRef="i_2026-01-05" unitRef="USDPerShare" name="us-gaap:CommonStockParOrStatedValuePerShare" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">0.0001</ix:nonFraction>. Following the effect of the <em style="font: inherit;">2026</em> Reverse Stock Split, each <ix:nonFraction id="c145591124" contextRef="d_2026-01-05_2026-01-05_NonmonetaryTransactionTypeAxis-ReverseStockSplitMember" unitRef="Pure" name="us-gaap:StockholdersEquityNoteStockSplitConversionRatio1" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">10</ix:nonFraction> shares of the Company&#8217;s common stock that were issued and outstanding automatically converted into <em style="font: inherit;">one</em> outstanding share of common stock.</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family: Times New Roman; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; text-align: justify; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt -1pt; text-indent: 26pt;">Combined, this had the effect of a <em style="font: inherit;">1</em>-for-<ix:nonFraction id="c145591127" contextRef="d_2024-06-07_2026-01-05_NonmonetaryTransactionTypeAxis-ReverseStockSplitMember" unitRef="Pure" name="us-gaap:StockholdersEquityNoteStockSplitConversionRatio1" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">250</ix:nonFraction> reverse stock split. All stock options and warrants of the Company outstanding immediately prior to each of the Reverse Stock Splits were proportionally adjusted except for the Listed Warrants and the private placement warrants that were issued as part of the Special Purpose Acquisition Corporation&#160;transaction that closed on <em style="font: inherit;"> October 28, 2022, </em>which total <ix:nonFraction id="c145591128" contextRef="i_2022-10-28_ClassOfWarrantOrRightAxis-UnadjustedWarrantsMember" unitRef="Share" name="us-gaap:ClassOfWarrantOrRightOutstanding" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">16,788,000</ix:nonFraction> outstanding warrants in the aggregate (the &#8220;Unadjusted Warrants&#8221;). The Unadjusted Warrants each retained an $<ix:nonFraction id="c145591129" contextRef="i_2022-10-28_ClassOfWarrantOrRightAxis-UnadjustedWarrantsMember" unitRef="USDPerShare" name="us-gaap:ClassOfWarrantOrRightExercisePriceOfWarrantsOrRights1" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">11.50</ix:nonFraction> exercise price and require the exercise of <span style="-sec-ix-hidden:c145591130">250</span> warrants to purchase <em style="font: inherit;">one</em> share of common stock. Unless otherwise indicated, all other share and per share amounts in this quarterly&#160;report reflect the effect of the Reverse Stock Splits. The par value of the Company&#8217;s common stock remained unchanged at $<ix:nonFraction id="c145591132" contextRef="i_2022-10-28" unitRef="USDPerShare" name="us-gaap:CommonStockParOrStatedValuePerShare" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">0.0001</ix:nonFraction> per share and the number of authorized shares of common stock remained the same after each of the Reverse Stock Splits.</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

        SeaStar Medical Holding Corporation

     <div class="hf-row">
      <div class="hf-cell" style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;, Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; margin: 0pt; width: calc(100% - 1px); display: inline-block;">

   <p style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;, Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; margin: 0pt; text-align: center;"><b><em style="font: inherit;"> March 31, 2026</em></b></p></ix:exclude><p style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;, Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; margin: 0pt; text-align: center;"><b><em style="font: inherit;"></em></b></p>
   <p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; margin: 0pt; text-align: left">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b><i>Segment Information</i></b></p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;text-indent:25pt;">The Company is comprised of a <span style="-sec-ix-hidden:c145591134">single</span> reportable segment, its Device Segment. This organizational structure aligns with how our Chief Operating Decision Maker (&#8220;CODM&#8221;), the Chief Executive Officer, manages the Company&#8217;s business, including resource allocation and performance assessment. The Company is focused entirely on the development, regulatory approval and commercialization of the Company&#8217;s adult and pediatric SCDs. The Company had a total of <ix:nonFraction id="c145591135" contextRef="i_2026-03-31" unitRef="Pure" name="icu:NumberOfEmployees" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">17</ix:nonFraction>&#160;employees at both&#160;&#160;<em style="font: inherit;"> March 31, 2026</em>&#160;and <em style="font: inherit;"> December 31, 2025, </em>and total assets of $<ix:nonFraction id="c145591136" contextRef="i_2026-03-31" unitRef="USD" name="us-gaap:Assets" scale="6" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-5">11.3</ix:nonFraction>&#160;million and $<ix:nonFraction id="c145591137" contextRef="i_2025-03-31" unitRef="USD" name="us-gaap:Assets" scale="6" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-5">14.2</ix:nonFraction>&#160;million, as of <em style="font: inherit;"> March 31, 2026</em>, and <em style="font: inherit;"> December 31, 2025</em>, respectively.</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;text-indent:25pt;">For segment reporting purposes, the CODM uses operating profit/(loss) to evaluate segment performance and allocate resources. As a Company that only recently began limited commercial sales of QUELIMMUNE, the CODM is primarily focused on evaluating the overall spending for research and development activities needed to fund further development of the SCDs, and general and administrative activities incurred to support the research and development activities of the Company. Accounting policies associated with the Company&#8217;s sole segment are the same as those described in Note <em style="font: inherit;">2.</em> See Note <em style="font: inherit;">14</em> for Segment Reporting information.</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family: Times New Roman; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; margin: 0pt; text-indent: 25pt; text-align: justify;">All of the Company&#8217;s sales are located within the United States. As of the date of this report,&#160;the Company has obtained regulatory approval for commercial sales in the U.S. of QUELIMMUNE&#160;from the FDA. The Company does <em style="font: inherit;">not</em> have any inter-entity sales or transfers.</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b><i>Liquidity and Going Concern</i></b></p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;text-indent:25pt;">The Company incurred losses of $<ix:nonFraction id="c145591141" contextRef="d_2026-01-01_2026-03-31" unitRef="USD" name="us-gaap:NetIncomeLoss" scale="6" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-5" sign="-">3.5</ix:nonFraction>&#160;million for the <em style="font: inherit;">three</em> months ended <em style="font: inherit;"> March 31, 2026</em>. As of <em style="font: inherit;"> March 31, 2026</em>, the Company has an accumulated deficit of $<ix:nonFraction id="c145591143" contextRef="i_2026-03-31" unitRef="USD" name="us-gaap:RetainedEarningsAccumulatedDeficit" scale="6" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-5" sign="-">155.2</ix:nonFraction>&#160;million and cash of $<ix:nonFraction id="c145591144" contextRef="i_2026-03-31" unitRef="USD" name="us-gaap:Cash" scale="6" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-5">9.3</ix:nonFraction>&#160;million. The Company does <em style="font: inherit;">not</em> believe that its cash on hand will be sufficient to enable it to fund its operations, including clinical trial expenses and capital expenditure requirements for at least <em style="font: inherit;">12</em> months from the issuance of these unaudited condensed consolidated financial statements. The Company believes that these conditions raise substantial doubt about its ability to continue as a going concern.</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;text-indent:25pt;">The Company&#8217;s need for additional capital will depend in part on the scope and costs of its development activities. To date, the Company has generated approximately $<ix:nonFraction id="c145591147" contextRef="d_2026-01-01_2026-03-31_ProductOrServiceAxis-QuelimmuneMember" unitRef="USD" name="us-gaap:RevenueFromContractWithCustomerExcludingAssessedTax" scale="6" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-5">1.9</ix:nonFraction> million&#160;revenue from the sales of its commercialized product, QUELIMMUNE to fund its operations. Its ability to generate meaningful product revenue will depend on the progress of the&#160;commercial launch of QUELIMMUNE and successful development and eventual commercialization of the adult SCD. Until such time, if ever, the Company&#160;expects to finance its operations through the sale of equity or debt securities, borrowing under credit facilities, or through potential collaborations, other strategic transactions or government and other grants. Adequate capital <em style="font: inherit;"> may </em><em style="font: inherit;">not</em> be available to the Company when needed or on acceptable terms.</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;text-indent:25pt;">If the Company is unable to raise capital, it could be forced to delay, reduce, suspend, or cease its research and development programs or any future commercialization efforts, which would have a negative impact on its business, prospects, operating results and financial condition. The accompanying unaudited condensed consolidated financial statements have been prepared assuming that the Company will continue as a going concern and do <em style="font: inherit;">not</em> include adjustments that might result from the outcome of this uncertainty. This basis of accounting contemplates the recovery of the Company&#8217;s assets and the satisfaction of liabilities in the normal course of business.</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b><i>Risks and Uncertainties</i></b></p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;text-indent:25pt;">The Company is subject to risks common to early-stage companies in the medical technology industry including, but <em style="font: inherit;">not</em> limited to, new medical and technological innovations, dependence on key personnel, protection of proprietary technology, and product liability. There can be <em style="font: inherit;">no</em> assurance that the Company&#8217;s products or services will be accepted in the marketplace, nor can there be any assurance that any future products or services can be developed or deployed at an acceptable cost and with appropriate performance characteristics, or that such products or services will be successfully marketed, if at all. These factors could have a materially adverse effect on the Company&#8217;s future financial results, financial position and cash flows.</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;text-indent:25pt;">&#160;</p>

  <ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2026-01-01_2026-03-31" name="us-gaap:SignificantAccountingPoliciesTextBlock" id="c3192792" escape="true">
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"><b>Note <em style="font: inherit;">2.</em></b>&#160;<b>Summary of Significant Accounting Policies</b></p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b><i></i></b></p><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2026-01-01_2026-03-31" name="us-gaap:UseOfEstimates" id="c145592416" escape="true"><p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b><i>Use of Estimates</i></b></p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;text-indent:25pt;">The preparation of the unaudited condensed consolidated financial statements in conformity with U.S. GAAP requires management to make estimates, assumptions and judgments that affect the reported amounts of assets and liabilities and disclosure of contingent assets and liabilities at the date of the unaudited condensed consolidated financial statements and reported amounts of revenues and expenses during the <em style="font: inherit;">three</em> months ended <em style="font: inherit;"> March 31, 2026. </em>Significant estimates include&#160;(i) accrued unbilled clinical trial costs, and (ii) stock-based compensation expense. Although actual results could differ from those estimates, such estimates are developed based on the best information available to management and management&#8217;s best judgments at the time.</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"></p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b><i></i></b></p><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2026-01-01_2026-03-31" name="us-gaap:ConcentrationRiskCreditRisk" id="c145592417" escape="true"><p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b><i>Concentrations of Credit Risk</i></b></p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;text-indent:25pt;">Financial instruments that potentially subject the Company to a significant concentration of credit risk consist primarily of cash. Periodically, the Company <em style="font: inherit;"> may </em>maintain deposits in financial institutions in excess of government insured limits. Any loss incurred or a lack of access to such funds could have a significant adverse impact on the Company&#8217;s financial condition, results of operations, and cash flows.</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"></p>
   <p style="font-family: Times New Roman; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt -1pt;"><b><i></i></b></p><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2026-01-01_2026-03-31" name="us-gaap:CashAndCashEquivalentsPolicyTextBlock" id="c145592418" escape="true"><p style="font-family: Times New Roman; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt -1pt;"><b><i>Cash and Cash Equivalents</i></b></p>
   <p style="font-family: Times New Roman; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt -1pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family: Times New Roman; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt -1pt; text-indent: 27pt;">The Company considers all highly liquid investments with an original maturity of <em style="font: inherit;">three</em> months or less to be cash equivalents. The Company did <span style="-sec-ix-hidden:c145591159"><span style="-sec-ix-hidden:c145591214">not</span></span> have any cash equivalents as of <em style="font: inherit;"> March 31, 2026 </em>and <em style="font: inherit;"> December 31, 2025.</em></p>
   <p style="font-family: Times New Roman; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; text-align: justify; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt -55pt; text-indent: 80pt;">&#160;</p></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font-family: Times New Roman; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; text-align: justify; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt -55pt; text-indent: 80pt;"></p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b><i></i></b></p><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2026-01-01_2026-03-31" name="us-gaap:TradeAndOtherAccountsReceivablePolicy" id="c145592419" escape="true"><p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b><i>Accounts Receivable, net</i></b></p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family: Times New Roman; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; text-align: justify; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt -1pt; text-indent: 26pt;">The Company recognizes accounts receivables from sales to customers at the time revenue is recognized and a customer invoice is created. The need for a credit loss allowance is evaluated each reporting period based on the Company&#8217;s assessment of the credit worthiness of its customers or any other potential circumstances that could result in a credit loss. The Company initially estimates credit losses based on a portfolio-wide method using an aging schedule at the end of each reporting period. As of <em style="font: inherit;"> March 31, 2026 </em>and <em style="font: inherit;"> December 31,&#160;</em><em style="font: inherit;">2025,</em> the Company had a current expected credit loss reserve of $<ix:nonFraction id="c145591163" contextRef="i_2026-03-31" unitRef="USD" name="us-gaap:AllowanceForDoubtfulAccountsReceivableCurrent" scale="3" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-3">1</ix:nonFraction>&#160;thousand and $<ix:nonFraction id="c145591165" contextRef="i_2025-12-31" unitRef="USD" name="us-gaap:AllowanceForDoubtfulAccountsReceivableCurrent" scale="3" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-3">3</ix:nonFraction> thousand, respectively.</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"></p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b><i></i></b></p><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2026-01-01_2026-03-31" name="us-gaap:FairValueOfFinancialInstrumentsPolicy" id="c145592420" escape="true"><p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b><i>Fair Value of Financial Instruments</i></b></p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family: Times New Roman; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; text-align: justify; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt -1pt; text-indent: 26pt;">The following provides a summary of those assets or liabilities for which the Company is required to measure at fair value either on a recurring basis, the valuation techniques and summary of inputs used to arrive at the measure of fair value. Changes in fair value of these assets or liabilities are recognized as a component of net income in the consolidated statements of operations. Changes in fair value of these assets or liabilities are considered unrealized gains or losses and therefore are classified as non-cash adjustments to reconcile net income to operating cash flows. Significant increases (decreases) in unobservable inputs used in fair value measurements could, in isolation, potentially result in a significantly lower or higher valuation for those assets or liabilities requiring recurring fair value measurements at each reporting date.</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt 0pt 0pt 8pt;text-indent:26pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family: Times New Roman; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; text-align: justify; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt -1pt; text-indent: 26pt;"><i>Liability Classified Warrants. </i>The Company has entered into or assumed various financial instruments, in the form of warrant agreements, that<i> </i>require classification as liabilities. This classification requires that the Company measure the warrants at each fair value at the end of each quarterly and annual reporting period.</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family: Times New Roman; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; text-align: justify; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt -1pt; text-indent: 26pt;">The Company uses a Black-Scholes option pricing model to fair value the warrants, using standard option pricing inputs such as the strike price of each warrant tranche, estimated volatility, time to maturity, and the risk-free interest rate. The risk-free interest rate is the U.S. Treasury rate at the date of issuance, and the time to maturity is based on the contractual life at the date of issuance, which is <em style="font: inherit;">five</em> years. The change in fair value of the liability classified warrants each reporting period is recorded to the change in fair value of warrants liability in the consolidated statements of operations.</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family: Times New Roman; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; text-align: justify; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt -1pt; text-indent: 26pt;"><i>Operating Current Assets and Current Liabilities. </i>The estimated fair value of cash, accounts receivables, prepaid expenses, accounts payable and<i> </i>accrued expenses approximate their fair value because of the short-term nature of these instruments.</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family: Times New Roman; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; text-align: justify; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt -1pt; text-indent: 26pt;"><i>Classification of Derivative Gains and Losses on the Consolidated Condensed Statement of Cash Flows. </i>Changes in fair value related to the Company&#8217;s<i> </i>derivative financial instruments consisting&#160;of liability classified warrants are&#160;recognized as a component of other&#160;income/(expense).</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <ix:exclude>

       <em style="font: inherit;"></em></div></div></div></ix:exclude></ix:nonNumeric><ix:exclude><div class="PGFTR" style="text-align: center; width: 100%"><div class="hf-row"><div class="hf-cell PGNUM" style="text-align: center; font-size: 10pt; font-family: Times New Roman;"><em style="font: inherit;">7</em>

        SeaStar Medical Holding Corporation
      </div></div></div></ix:exclude><ix:exclude><div class="PGBK" style="width: 100%; margin-left: 0pt; margin-right: 0pt"><div class="PGHDR" style="width: 100%;"><div class="hf-row"><div class="hf-cell" style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;, Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; margin: 0pt; width: calc(100% - 1px); display: inline-block;"></div>

   <p style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;, Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; margin: 0pt; text-align: center;"><b><em style="font: inherit;"> March 31, 2026</em></b></p></ix:exclude><p style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;, Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; margin: 0pt; text-align: center;"><b><em style="font: inherit;"></em></b></p>
   <p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; margin: 0pt; text-align: left">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b><i></i></b></p><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2026-01-01_2026-03-31" name="us-gaap:RevenueRecognitionPolicyTextBlock" id="c145592421" escape="true"><p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b><i>Revenue Recognition </i></b></p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><i>Overall</i></p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;text-indent:25pt;">Under ASC <em style="font: inherit;">606,</em> <i>Revenues from Contracts with Customers </i>("Topic <em style="font: inherit;">606"</em>) the Company recognizes revenue when a customer obtains control of promised goods or services, in an amount that reflects the consideration that the Company expects to receive in exchange for those goods or services. To determine revenue recognition for arrangements that the Company determines are within the scope of Topic <em style="font: inherit;">606,</em> the Company evaluates the following criteria: (i) identify the contract with a customer; (ii) identify the performance obligations in the contract; (iii) determine the transaction price, including variable consideration, if any; (iv) allocate the transaction price to the performance obligations in the contract; and (v) recognize revenue when (or as) performance obligations are satisfied.</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;text-indent:25pt;">At contract inception, once the contract is determined to be within the scope of Topic <em style="font: inherit;">606,</em> the Company assesses whether the goods or services promised within each contract are distinct and, therefore, represent a separate performance obligation. Goods and services that are determined <em style="font: inherit;">not</em> to be distinct are combined with other promised goods and services until a distinct combined performance obligation is identified. The Company then allocates the transaction price to each performance obligation and recognizes the associated revenue when (or as) each performance obligation is satisfied. The estimate of the transaction price for each contract includes all variable consideration to which the Company expects to be entitled, subject to the constraint on variable consideration. The Company constrains revenue by giving consideration to factors that could otherwise lead to a probable reversal of revenue. Variable consideration is <em style="font: inherit;">not</em> constrained if the potential reversal of cumulative revenue recognized at the contract level is <em style="font: inherit;">not</em> significant.</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;text-indent:25pt;">The Company currently does <em style="font: inherit;">not</em> receive customer deposits or prepayments&#160;prior to the Company fulfilling its performance obligation(s) under each&#160;contract obligation(s).&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><i>Product Sales Revenue</i></p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;text-indent:25pt;">The Company sells its products directly to end-user qualified customers using the Company&#8217;s own internal commercial/sales resources.</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="width: 100%; text-indent: 0px;"><tbody><tr style="vertical-align: top;"><td style="width: 27pt;">&#160;</td><td style="width: 27pt;"> <p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif;font-size: 10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#9679;</p> </td><td style="width: auto;"> <p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; margin: 0pt; text-align: justify;"><i>Timing of Revenue Recognition</i>&#160;&#8211;&#160;During the brief history (commenced <em style="font: inherit;"> July 2024) </em>of selling QUELIMMUNE, revenue has been recognized based on a <i>freight-on-board destination (</i>&#8220;FOB Destination&#8221;) requirement, except in limited cases where they are sold <i>freight-on-board shipping point</i>&#160;(&#8220;FOB Shipping Point&#8221;).</p> </td></tr>
</tbody></table>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="width: 100%; text-indent: 0px;"><tbody><tr style="vertical-align: top;"><td style="width: 27pt;">&#160;</td><td style="width: 27pt;"> <p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif;font-size: 10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#9679;</p> </td><td style="width: auto;"> <p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; margin: 0pt; text-align: justify;"><i>Chargebacks, Government Rebates and Discounts</i>&#160;&#8211;&#160;During the brief history of selling QUELIMMUNE commercially, the Company has <em style="font: inherit;">not</em> agreed to chargebacks, government rebates or discounts.</p> </td></tr>
</tbody></table>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="width: 100%; text-indent: 0px;"><tbody><tr style="vertical-align: top;"><td style="width: 27pt;">&#160;</td><td style="width: 27pt;"> <p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif;font-size: 10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#9679;</p> </td><td style="width: auto;"> <p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; margin: 0pt; text-align: justify;"><i>Returns</i>&#160;&#8211;&#160;Returns are specific to each order, but generally the Company allows for returns of any damaged or non-conforming product within <em style="font: inherit;">30</em> days of receipt of product. Given the (i) overall rate of product shipped that is defective/damaged, (ii) overall volume of sales to individual end-user customers, (iii) expected supply in the customer channel, and (iv) expected usage by customers, the Company does <em style="font: inherit;">not</em> anticipate that there will be significant risk of material product returns that require recognition.</p> </td></tr>
</tbody></table>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="width: 100%; text-indent: 0px;"><tbody><tr style="vertical-align: top;"><td style="width: 27pt;">&#160;</td><td style="width: 27pt;"> <p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif;font-size: 10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#9679;</p> </td><td style="width: auto;"> <p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; margin: 0pt; text-align: justify;"><i>Variable Consideration</i>&#160;&#8211;&#160;There are currently <em style="font: inherit;">no</em> other variable consideration elements outside of those already disclosed in this footnote.</p> </td></tr>
</tbody></table>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="width: 100%; text-indent: 0px;"><tbody><tr style="vertical-align: top;"><td style="width: 27pt;">&#160;</td><td style="width: 27pt;"> <p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif;font-size: 10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#9679;</p> </td><td style="width: auto;"> <p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; margin: 0pt; text-align: justify;"><i>Transaction Price</i>&#160;&#8211;&#160;Based on the above, as currently constructed, the Company&#8217;s transaction price is fixed, based on the agreed-upon price per each purchase order submitted by each customer. There are <em style="font: inherit;">no</em> milestone or up-front payments.&#160;</p> </td></tr>
</tbody></table>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="width: 100%; text-indent: 0px;"><tbody><tr style="vertical-align: top;"><td style="width: 27pt;">&#160;</td><td style="width: 27pt;"> <p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif;font-size: 10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#9679;</p> </td><td style="width: auto;"> <p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; margin: 0pt; text-align: justify;"><i>Allocation of Consideration</i>&#160;&#8211;&#160;Each sale of QUELIMMUNE is independent of any and all other sales. The entire transaction price for each QUELIMMUNE unit sold is allocated to that unit of QUELIMMUNE, and there are <em style="font: inherit;">no</em> allocations to services or other performance obligations, as there are <em style="font: inherit;">no</em> such services or other performance obligations that require the Company to allocate a portion of the transaction price.</p> </td></tr>
</tbody></table>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

     <div class="hf-row">
      <div class="hf-cell" style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;, Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; margin: 0pt; width: calc(100% - 1px); display: inline-block;">

   <p style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;, Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; margin: 0pt; text-align: center;"><b><em style="font: inherit;"> March 31, 2026</em></b></p>
   <p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; margin: 0pt; text-align: left">&#160;</p></ix:exclude><p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; margin: 0pt; text-align: left"></p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;text-indent:25pt;">The Company will continue to monitor all of the above as the Company continues to commercialize and increase its customer base, which could result in each distributor or end-user customer agreement having its own unique terms and conditions, that will potentially impact the timing and amount of revenue recognition pursuant to U.S. GAAP.</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"></p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b><i></i></b></p><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2026-01-01_2026-03-31" name="us-gaap:CostOfSalesPolicyTextBlock" id="c145592422" escape="true"><p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b><i>Cost of Goods Sold</i></b></p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;text-indent:25pt;">Prior to <em style="font: inherit;"> July 2024, </em>the Company manufactured/assembled QUELIMMUNE and adult SCDs only for research oriented and/or clinical trial related activities. Inventory purchased prior to <em style="font: inherit;"> July 2024 </em>was expensed as a period expense at the time of purchase as a research and development expense. Accordingly, all QUELIMMUNE units sold prior to the <em style="font: inherit;">three</em> months ended <em style="font: inherit;"> June 30, 2025, </em>had <em style="font: inherit;">no</em> recognized inventory value. During the <em style="font: inherit;">three</em>&#160;months ended <em style="font: inherit;"> March 31, 2026</em>&#160;,&#160;the Company recognized approximately $<ix:nonFraction id="c145591191" contextRef="d_2026-01-01_2026-03-31" unitRef="USD" name="us-gaap:CostOfGoodsAndServicesSold" scale="3" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-3">46</ix:nonFraction>&#160;thousand for cost of goods sold.</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;text-indent:25pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;text-indent:25pt;">The Company purchases supplies for the production of QUELIMMUNE and adult SCDs, some of which is used in the production of both. The Company's policy for the accounting for these <em style="font: inherit;">three</em> categories of inventory is as follows:</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="width:100%;font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;"><tbody><tr><td style="width:34pt;">&#160;</td><td style="vertical-align:top;width:18pt;"> <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#9679;</p> </td><td style="vertical-align:top;"> <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"><i>QUELIMMUNE Specific Inventory </i>&#8211;<i>&#160;</i>comprises raw materials used solely for the assembly of QUELIMMUNE inventory. It will be recognized to<i>&#160;</i>inventory as either raw materials, work-in-process, or finished goods depending on the stage of assembly. It will be charged to cost of goods sold upon shipment to a customer.</p> </td></tr>
</tbody></table>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="width:100%;font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;"><tbody><tr><td style="width:34pt;">&#160;</td><td style="vertical-align:top;width:18pt;"> <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#9679;</p> </td><td style="vertical-align:top;"> <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"><i>Commingled Inventory </i>&#8211;<i>&#160;</i>comprises raw materials that are used both for the assembly of QUELIMMUNE or adult SCDs. If used in the assembly<i>&#160;</i>of QUELIMMUNE, it will continue to be included in inventory as either work-in-process or finished goods depending on the stage of assembly and charged to cost of goods sold upon shipment to a customer.</p> </td></tr>
</tbody></table>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="width:100%;font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;"><tbody><tr><td style="width:34pt;">&#160;</td><td style="vertical-align:top;width:18pt;"> <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#9679;</p> </td><td style="vertical-align:top;"> <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"><i>Adult SCD Specific Inventory </i>&#8211;<i>&#160;</i>comprises raw materials used solely for the assembly of adult SCDs primarily to fulfill the demands of the<i>&#160;</i>NEUTRALIZE-AKI study. These supplies will be charged to research and development expense upon acquisition, until such time, that it is probable that these supplies could be assembled into adult SCD kits to be sold commercially.</p> </td></tr>
</tbody></table>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"></p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b><i></i></b></p><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2026-01-01_2026-03-31" name="us-gaap:ResearchAndDevelopmentExpensePolicy" id="c145592423" escape="true"><p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b><i>Funded Research &amp; Development Expense</i></b></p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family: Times New Roman; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; margin: 0pt; text-indent: 25pt; text-align: justify;">During the year-ended <em style="font: inherit;"> December 31, 2025,&#160;</em>the Company entered into an agreement to provide contract research services to an outside party, which involves a study of the Company's selective cytopheretic device relating to patients with severe, chronic heart failure. The Company is able to bill the outside party for a certain portion of the costs incurred to provide these services. The Company will own and is able to benefit from the knowledge gained from the results of this study. Accordingly, the Company will account for any funds paid by the outside party to the Company as a reimbursed expense, in accordance with ASC <em style="font: inherit;">730</em>-<em style="font: inherit;">20</em> as a<i> </i>funded research and development arrangement. Accordingly, any amounts incurred and billed will offset the Company's operating expenses categorized as research and development expense on the Company&#8217;s condensed consolidated statements of operations for the <em style="font: inherit;">three</em> months ended <em style="font: inherit;"> March 31, 2026 </em>and <em style="font: inherit;">2025.</em>&#160;The reimbursed expenses for the <em style="font: inherit;">three</em> months ended <em style="font: inherit;"> March 31, 2026 </em>and <em style="font: inherit;">2025</em>&#160;totaled approximately $<ix:nonFraction id="c145591199" contextRef="d_2026-01-01_2026-03-31" unitRef="USD" name="icu:FundedResearchAndDevelopmentReimbursedExpenses" scale="3" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-3">39</ix:nonFraction> thousand and $<ix:nonFraction id="c145591200" contextRef="d_2025-01-01_2025-03-31" unitRef="USD" name="icu:FundedResearchAndDevelopmentReimbursedExpenses" scale="3" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-3">0</ix:nonFraction>, respectively.&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"></p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b><i></i></b></p><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2026-01-01_2026-03-31" name="icu:EmergingGrowthCompanyStatusPolicyTextBlock" id="c145592424" escape="true"><p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b><i>Emerging Growth Company Status</i></b></p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;text-indent:25pt;">The Company is an &#8220;emerging growth company&#8221;, as defined in the Jumpstart Our Business Startups Act of <em style="font: inherit;">2012</em> (&#8220;JOBS Act&#8221;). Under the JOBS Act, emerging growth companies can take advantage of an extended transition period for complying with new or revised accounting standards, delaying the adoption of these accounting standards until they apply to private companies. The Company has elected to use this extended transition period for complying with certain new or revised accounting standards that have different effective dates for public and private companies until the earlier of the date that it is (<em style="font: inherit;">1</em>) <em style="font: inherit;">no</em> longer an emerging growth company or (<em style="font: inherit;">2</em>) affirmatively and irrevocably opt out of the extended transition period provided in the JOBS Act.</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"></p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b><i></i></b></p><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2026-01-01_2026-03-31" name="us-gaap:NewAccountingPronouncementsPolicyPolicyTextBlock" id="c145592425" escape="true"><p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b><i>Recently issued Accounting Standards</i></b></p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;text-indent:25pt;"><i>Accounting Standards Update <em style="font: inherit;">2024</em>-<em style="font: inherit;">03</em> </i>&#8212;<i> </i>In <em style="font: inherit;"> November 2024, </em>the FASB issued ASU <em style="font: inherit;">2024</em>-<em style="font: inherit;">03</em> - <i>Income Statement - Reporting Comprehensive Income </i>&#8211;<i> Expense Disaggregation (Subtopic <em style="font: inherit;">220</em>-<em style="font: inherit;">40</em>): Disaggregation of Income Statement Expenses</i>. ASU <em style="font: inherit;">2024</em>-<em style="font: inherit;">03</em> requires the disclosure of additional information related to certain costs and expenses, including amounts of inventory purchases, employee compensation, and depreciation and amortization included in each income statement line item. The ASU is effective for annual periods beginning after <em style="font: inherit;"> December 15, 2026, </em>and interim periods beginning after <em style="font: inherit;"> December 15, 2027, </em>with early adoption permitted. The Company is currently evaluating the impact of adopting this guidance on its consolidated financial statements and disclosures.</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"></p>

   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b>Note <em style="font: inherit;">3.</em> Revenues and Contract Obligations</b></p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; margin: 0pt; text-indent: 27pt;">The Company recognized $<ix:nonFraction id="c145591218" contextRef="d_2026-01-01_2026-03-31" unitRef="USD" name="us-gaap:RevenueFromContractWithCustomerExcludingAssessedTax" scale="6" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-5">0.5</ix:nonFraction> million and $<ix:nonFraction id="c145591219" contextRef="d_2025-01-01_2025-03-31" unitRef="USD" name="us-gaap:RevenueFromContractWithCustomerExcludingAssessedTax" scale="6" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-5">0.3</ix:nonFraction> million in revenues for the <em style="font: inherit;">three</em> months ended <em style="font: inherit;"> March 31, 2026 </em>and <em style="font: inherit;">2025,</em> respectively.&#160;The Company&#8217;s sales are entirely in the United States, as it does <em style="font: inherit;">not</em> have any regulatory approval to sell elsewhere as of <em style="font: inherit;"> March 31, 2026</em>.</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"><i>Contract Assets and Liabilities</i></p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; margin: 0pt; text-indent: 27pt;">There are and were <em style="font: inherit;">no</em> contract assets or liabilities as of and at any time during the <em style="font: inherit;">three</em> months ended <em style="font: inherit;"> March 31, 2026 </em>and <em style="font: inherit;">2025,</em> respectively. This is because&#160;the Company's current contracts with customers do <em style="font: inherit;">not</em> contain features, terms or conditions that would give rise to such contract assets or liabilities.&#160;&#160;&#160;</p>
  </ix:nonNumeric>
  <div>

   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"><b>Note <em style="font: inherit;">6.</em> Notes Payable</b></p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b><i>Insurance Financing</i></b></p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;text-indent:25pt;">In <em style="font: inherit;"> October 2025, </em>the Company entered into a financing arrangement with a lender to finance a portion of the annual premium of an insurance policy in the amount of $<ix:nonFraction id="c145591255" contextRef="i_2025-10-31_DebtInstrumentAxis-FinancingOfInsurancePolicyPremiumsMember" unitRef="USD" name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentFaceAmount" scale="6" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-5">0.7</ix:nonFraction> million, to be paid over <em style="font: inherit;">ten</em> months, with the final payment in <em style="font: inherit;"> August 2026.&#160;</em>As of <em style="font: inherit;"> March 31, 2026</em>, the Company owed approximately $<ix:nonFraction id="c145591257" contextRef="i_2026-03-31_DebtInstrumentAxis-FinancingOfInsurancePolicyPremiumsMember" unitRef="USD" name="us-gaap:NotesPayableCurrent" scale="6" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-5">0.3</ix:nonFraction> million under this arrangement.&#160;&#160; &#160;&#160;</p>
  </ix:nonNumeric>
  <div>
    &#160;
  </div>
  <ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2026-01-01_2026-03-31" name="us-gaap:StockholdersEquityNoteDisclosureTextBlock" id="c3192799" escape="true">
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"><b>Note <em style="font: inherit;">7.</em> Equity Transactions</b></p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"><b><i>Standby Equity Purchase Agreement </i></b></p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family: Times New Roman; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; text-align: justify; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 2pt; text-indent: 24pt;">On <em style="font: inherit;"> April 25, 2025, </em>the Company entered into a standby equity purchase agreement (&#8220;Common Stock Purchase Agreement&#8221;) and related registration rights agreement (the &#8220;Registration Rights Agreement&#8221;) with Lincoln Park Capital Fund, LLC (&#8220;Lincoln Park&#8221;), collectively the &#8220;SEPA&#8221;. Pursuant to the Common Stock Purchase Agreement, the Company has the right, but <em style="font: inherit;">not</em> the obligation, to direct Lincoln Park to purchase up to $<ix:nonFraction id="c145591261" contextRef="d_2025-04-25_2025-04-25_CounterpartyNameAxis-LincolnParkCapitalFundLlcMember_RangeAxis-MaximumMember_SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis-StandbyEquityPurchaseAgreementMember" unitRef="USD" name="icu:ShareIssuableMaximumValue" scale="6" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-6">15.0</ix:nonFraction> million in aggregate gross purchase price of newly issued shares of Common Stock, subject to certain limitations and conditions as described below (the &#8220;SEPA Program&#8221;) at a purchase price equal to <ix:nonFraction id="c145591262" contextRef="i_2025-04-25_CounterpartyNameAxis-LincolnParkCapitalFundLlcMember_SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis-StandbyEquityPurchaseAgreementMember" unitRef="Pure" name="icu:ShareIssuedPricePerSharePercentageOfSalePrice" scale="-2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="2">97</ix:nonFraction>% of the lesser of (i) the lowest sale price of the Common Stock on the purchase date or (ii) average of the <em style="font: inherit;">three</em> lowest closing sale prices of the Common Stock over the last <em style="font: inherit;">ten</em> business days prior to the purchase date.</p>
   <p style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt -9pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family: Times New Roman; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; text-align: justify; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 2pt; text-indent: 24pt;">The Company controls the timing and amount of any sales to Lincoln Park, which depend on a variety of factors including, among other things, market conditions, the trading price of the Company&#8217;s common stock, and determinations by the Company as to appropriate sources of funding for its business and operations. However, Lincoln Park&#8217;s obligation to purchase shares is subject to certain conditions, including the daily trading volume of the Company&#8217;s stock. In all instances, the Company <em style="font: inherit;"> may </em><em style="font: inherit;">not</em> sell shares of Common Stock under the Purchase Agreement if it would result in Lincoln Park and its affiliate beneficially owning more than <ix:nonFraction id="c145591266" contextRef="i_2025-04-25_CounterpartyNameAxis-LincolnParkCapitalFundLlcMember_SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis-StandbyEquityPurchaseAgreementMember" unitRef="Pure" name="icu:OwnershipOfOutstandingVotingPowerPercentage" scale="-2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="4">9.99</ix:nonFraction>% of outstanding voting power or shares of the Common Stock at any <em style="font: inherit;">one</em> point in time.</p>
   <p style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt -9pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family: Times New Roman; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; text-align: justify; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 2pt; text-indent: 24pt;">As part of the SEPA, the Company agreed to issue to&#160;Lincoln Park <ix:nonFraction id="c145591268" contextRef="d_2025-04-25_2025-04-25_CounterpartyNameAxis-LincolnParkCapitalFundLlcMember_SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis-StandbyEquityPurchaseAgreementMember" unitRef="Share" name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">23,641</ix:nonFraction> shares of the Company&#8217;s Common Stock, valued at approximately $<ix:nonFraction id="c145591269" contextRef="d_2025-04-25_2025-04-25_CounterpartyNameAxis-LincolnParkCapitalFundLlcMember_SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis-StandbyEquityPurchaseAgreementMember" unitRef="USD" name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodValueNewIssues" scale="6" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-5">0.3</ix:nonFraction> million on the date of issuance, <em style="font: inherit;"> April 25, 2025 (</em>the &#8220;Commitment Fee&#8221;).</p>
   <p style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt -9pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family: Times New Roman; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; text-align: justify; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 2pt; text-indent: 24pt;">The Company evaluated the contract that includes the right to require Lincoln Park to purchase shares of Common Stock in the future (&#8220;put right&#8221;) considering the guidance in ASC <em style="font: inherit;">815</em>-<em style="font: inherit;">40,</em> <i>Derivatives and Hedging</i> &#8212; <i>Contracts on an Entity</i>&#8217;<i>s Own Equity</i> and concluded that it is an equity-linked contract that does <em style="font: inherit;">not</em> qualify for equity classification, and therefore requires fair value accounting. The Company analyzed the terms of the freestanding put right and concluded that it has an immaterial value at the issuance date of <em style="font: inherit;"> April 25, 2025, </em>and as of <em style="font: inherit;"> March 31, 2026 </em>and&#160;<em style="font: inherit;"> December 31, 2025.</em></p>
   <p style="font-family: Times New Roman; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; text-align: justify; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 2pt; text-indent: 24pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family: Times New Roman; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; text-align: justify; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 2pt; text-indent: 24pt;">Through <em style="font: inherit;"> March 31, 2026, </em>the Company raised approximately $<ix:nonFraction id="c145591273" contextRef="d_2025-04-25_2026-03-31_SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis-StandbyEquityPurchaseAgreementMember" unitRef="USD" name="us-gaap:ProceedsFromIssuanceOrSaleOfEquity" scale="6" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-5">0.3</ix:nonFraction> million, net of offering costs of approximately $<ix:nonFraction id="c145591274" contextRef="d_2025-04-25_2026-03-31_SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis-StandbyEquityPurchaseAgreementMember" unitRef="USD" name="us-gaap:PaymentsOfStockIssuanceCosts" scale="3" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-3">18</ix:nonFraction> thousand,&#160;issuing approximately <ix:nonFraction id="c145591275" contextRef="d_2025-04-25_2026-03-31_SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis-StandbyEquityPurchaseAgreementMember" unitRef="Share" name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" scale="6" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-6">138,184</ix:nonFraction>&#160;million&#160;shares of the Company's Common Stock. During the <em style="font: inherit;">three</em> months ended <em style="font: inherit;"> March 31, 2026, </em>the Company raised $<ix:nonFraction id="c145591277" contextRef="d_2026-01-01_2026-03-31_SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis-StandbyEquityPurchaseAgreementMember" unitRef="USD" name="us-gaap:ProceedsFromIssuanceOrSaleOfEquity" scale="6" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-5">0.3</ix:nonFraction> million, net of offering costs of approximately $<ix:nonFraction id="c145591278" contextRef="d_2026-01-01_2026-03-31_SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis-StandbyEquityPurchaseAgreementMember" unitRef="USD" name="us-gaap:PaymentsOfStockIssuanceCosts" scale="3" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-3">16</ix:nonFraction> thousand, issuing <ix:nonFraction id="c145591279" contextRef="d_2026-01-01_2026-03-31_SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis-StandbyEquityPurchaseAgreementMember" unitRef="Share" name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" scale="6" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-6">130,184</ix:nonFraction>&#160;million shares of the Company's Common Stock.</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

   <p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; margin: 0pt; text-align: left">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b><i>At-The-Market Offering</i></b></p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;text-indent:25pt;">On <em style="font: inherit;"> August 20, 2024, </em>the Company entered into an At-The-Market Offering Agreement (the &#8220;ATM Agreement&#8221;) with Wainwright as sales agent, to sell shares of its common stock, from time to time, through an &#8220;at the market offering&#8221; program under which Wainwright will act as sales agent. The sales, if any, of the Company&#8217;s Common Stock made under the ATM Agreement will be made by any method permitted by law deemed to be an &#8220;at the market offering&#8221; as defined in Rule <em style="font: inherit;">415</em> promulgated under the Securities Act of <em style="font: inherit;">1933,</em> as amended (the &#8220;Securities Act&#8221;), including sales made directly on or through the Nasdaq Capital Market or on any other existing trading market for the Company&#8217;s common stock (the &#8220;ATM&#8221;).</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;text-indent:25pt;">Through <em style="font: inherit;"> March 31, 2026</em>, the Company has raised approximately $<ix:nonFraction id="c145591283" contextRef="d_2024-08-20_2026-03-31_SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis-AtthemarketAgreementMember" unitRef="USD" name="us-gaap:ProceedsFromIssuanceOrSaleOfEquity" scale="6" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-5">10.4</ix:nonFraction>&#160;million, net of offering costs of approximately $<ix:nonFraction id="c145591284" contextRef="d_2024-08-20_2026-03-31_SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis-AtthemarketAgreementMember" unitRef="USD" name="us-gaap:PaymentsOfStockIssuanceCosts" scale="6" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-5">0.4</ix:nonFraction> million,&#160;utilizing the ATM since inception in <em style="font: inherit;"> August 2024, </em>issuing approximately <ix:nonFraction id="c145591285" contextRef="d_2024-08-20_2026-03-31_SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis-AtthemarketAgreementMember" unitRef="Share" name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" scale="6" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-6">1,081,207</ix:nonFraction>&#160;shares of the Company&#8217;s Common Stock. During the <em style="font: inherit;">three</em> months ended <em style="font: inherit;"> March 31, 2026</em>, the Company raised approximately $<ix:nonFraction id="c145591287" contextRef="d_2026-01-01_2026-03-31_SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis-AtthemarketAgreementMember" unitRef="USD" name="us-gaap:ProceedsFromIssuanceOrSaleOfEquity" scale="6" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-5">0.1</ix:nonFraction>&#160;million, net of offering costs of approximately $<ix:nonFraction id="c145591288" contextRef="d_2026-01-01_2026-03-31_SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis-AtthemarketAgreementMember" unitRef="USD" name="us-gaap:PaymentsOfStockIssuanceCosts" scale="6" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-6">2</ix:nonFraction> thousand, issuing approximately <ix:nonFraction id="c145591289" contextRef="d_2026-01-01_2026-03-31_SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis-AtthemarketAgreementMember" unitRef="Share" name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" scale="6" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-7">18,880</ix:nonFraction>&#160;shares&#160;of the Company's Common Stock.</p>

   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"><b><i>Equity Incentive Plan - Summary</i></b></p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"><b><i><em style="font: inherit;">2022</em> Omnibus Incentive Plan</i></b></p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;text-indent:25pt;">The Company&#8217;s Board of Directors adopted, and the stockholders approved the <em style="font: inherit;">2022</em> Omnibus Incentive Plan to provide long-term incentive for its employees and non-employee service providers. The vesting of stock options is stated in each individual grant agreement, which is generally <span style="-sec-ix-hidden:c145591404">one</span> to <span style="-sec-ix-hidden:c145591405">four</span> years. Options granted expire <ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2026-01-01_2026-03-31_AwardTypeAxis-EmployeeStockOptionMember_PlanNameAxis-The2022OmnibusEquityIncentivePlanMember" name="us-gaap:SharebasedCompensationArrangementBySharebasedPaymentAwardExpirationPeriod" id="c145591406" format="ixt-sec:duryear">10</ix:nonNumeric> years after the date of grant.</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"><b><i><em style="font: inherit;">2019</em> Stock Incentive Plan</i></b></p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
   <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;text-indent:25pt;">The Company&#8217;s Board of Directors adopted the <em style="font: inherit;">2019</em> Stock Incentive Plan on <em style="font: inherit;"> February 25, 2019, </em>to provide long-term incentive for its employees and non-employee service providers. The Stock Incentive Plan was terminated on <em style="font: inherit;"> October 28, 2022, </em>and <em style="font: inherit;">no</em> further awards were granted under such plan.</p>
   <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
